- **notes**: - The main checkout is a GitButler workspace: every commit goes through `but` there (`but diff`, then `but commit -b &lt;branch&gt; -m "&lt;msg&gt;" &lt;file-ids&gt;`), never plain `git commit`. - **notes-critic**: Adversarial QA agent that checks Lecture Notes (article-class .tex) against the Beamer deck it was expanded from. Checks nothing was invented, nothing was dropped, and every citation/page-pointer is preserved. Does NOT edit files — read-only analysis only. ([AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine](https://github.com/AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notes-critic.md))
- **notes-extractor**: Read-only gatherer for ax-report. Given a date window and scope, scans the notes repo (notes, plans, tasks, daily files, INDEX.md, PRS.md, TASKS.md) via frontmatter and ledger rows and returns one compact JSON document — one item per in-window file with its PRs (plus ledger status), Jira keys, tasks, a one-line outcome and any Cost figures, plus counts and the earliest recorded date. Never writes, never calls GitHub or Jira, never invents items that aren't backed by a file or ledger row. ([garrefa/ai-notes](https://github.com/garrefa/ai-notes/blob/HEAD/agents/notes-extractor.md) · ★2)
- **notes-fixer**: Implements fixes from the notes-critic agent. Applies changes to Lecture Notes (.tex) files. Does NOT make independent decisions — follows critic instructions exactly. ([AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine](https://github.com/AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notes-fixer.md))

- **nothing-new-under-the-sun**: There Is Nothing New Under The Sun — a read-only scouting agent. Searches existing solutions (codebase, dependencies, packages, GitHub repos, APIs, SaaS, templates) before anyone writes code. Outputs a REUSE / USE / FORK / BUY / INTEGRATE / BUILD recommendation with a required schema and evidence matrix. Uses five-component long-term business value framing. Never writes code, never installs packages, never edits files. Use BEFORE build-vs-buy decisions, new dependencies, new systems, or greenfield features. Skip bug fixes, small refactors, docs, style/lint, and imports already in use. - **novel-forge-critic**: 小说反套路审查官。严苛审查正文中的套路、AI痕迹、逻辑漏洞，输出问题清单。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to review chapter text. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-critic.md))
- **novel-forge-improver**: 小说剧情优化师。根据Critic的问题清单逐条修复正文中的套路、AI味、逻辑问题。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to fix critic issues. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-improver.md))
- **novel-forge-memory**: 小说记忆系统。QA通过后更新章节记忆文件和全局记忆。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to update story memory after chapter passes QA. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-memory.md))
- **novel-forge-planner**: 小说章节规划师。根据大纲、全局记忆、前章记忆生成本章详细写作计划。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to plan a chapter. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-planner.md))
- **novel-forge-publisher**: 小说发布运营编辑。QA通过后生成作者有话说和发布物料。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to generate publishing materials. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-publisher.md))
- **novel-forge-qa**: 小说质量审查员。对最终正文执行硬性质量检查：禁词扫描、字数统计、标点检查、连贯性验证。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator for final quality check. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-qa.md))
- **novel-forge-styler**: 小说文风打磨师。深度去AI化，优化节奏感、句式变化、口语化程度，让文字读起来像真人网文作者。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to polish writing style. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-styler.md))
- **novel-forge-writer**: 小说正文执行写手。根据Planner的章节计划写2000-4000字中文网文正文。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to write chapter content. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-writer.md))
- **novel-generator**: Activate when the user asks to write or continue a novel, generate fiction, draft serialized chapters, expand a story idea into a prompt or outline, or mentions 爽文/小说/写作/网文 — especially Chinese genres like 都市、修仙、玄幻、重生、系统流、末世、网游. Also use when the user wants continuity tracking (characters, locations, plot points) across a long-form work. ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/novel-generator/agents/novel-generator.md) · ★455 · 2 copies)
- **novel-generator**: CV → Tabular: MixUp/CutMix for tabular data (blend rows for augmentation) NLP → Tabular: Treat categorical sequences as "sentences," use embeddings Time Series → NLP: Treat text as time series of tokens, apply forecasting ideas RL → Optimization: Treat hyperparameter search as a bandit problem Physics → ML: Conservation laws as constraints, symmetry exploitation Economics → ML: Instrumental variab ([ankitjha67/competitive-dominator](https://github.com/ankitjha67/competitive-dominator/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-generator.md) · ★2)
- **novel-line-rewriter**: 文字改稿 + 「去 AI 味」专家。专长对 🔴 必改项执行改稿（扫描由 `novel-line-scanner` 完成）。先备份原稿到 `.cursorGrowth/archive/`，改稿覆盖正文文件，报告写到 `.cursorGrowth/check/第NN章_第SS节_复盘.md`。**不动白名单**（母题数字 / 关键概念 / 重锤词的 `**`）、**不动对白断句 / 潜台词**、**不擅改母题 / 情节 / 人物**。用于"去 AI 味"/"修一下"/"改稿"。 ([wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel](https://github.com/wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/novel-line-rewriter.agent.md))
- **novel-line-scanner**: 文字扫描 + 八股检测专家。只扫描不改稿。专长 9 维八股检测（AI 八股 / 翻译腔 / 形容词堆砌 / 加粗密度 / 被动句 / Show vs Tell / 节奏 / 感官 / 分镜式二级标题），输出三档批注（🔴/🟡/🟢）。用于"扫一下"/"看一下"/"八股检测"以及 `novel-check` 一致性检查。**不动原文件**，所有批注写到 `.cursorGrowth/check/`。 ([wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel](https://github.com/wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/novel-line-scanner.agent.md))
- **novel-lorekeeper**: Owns a kt novel project's glossary.md and style.md. Builds them from the Korean before translation starts, calibrates them against the first translated chapters, resolves glossary conflicts and pronoun questions, and folds copyedit lessons back in. Dispatched by the /translate-novel skill with a slug and a mode (bootstrap, calibrate, resolve, update, lessons). ([beadvd/ktrans](https://github.com/beadvd/ktrans/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-lorekeeper.md))
- **novel-lorekeeper**: 世界观守护者，被动型Agent。负责管理人物数据库、时间线、伏笔状态，确保所有设定的一致性和可追溯性。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-lorekeeper.md))
- **novel-memory**: 长篇小说记忆体（连续性编辑）。负责上下文摘要维护、人物状态追踪、情节线索归档、跨对话连续性管理。当需要获取当前稿件状态快照、追踪未解决情节线、或在新对话中恢复上下文时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-memory.md) · ★6)
- **novel-outline-architect**: Use this agent when the user needs to analyze, deconstruct, or improve the outline (大纲) or detailed outline (细纲) of a novel. This includes deconstructing successful novels of any genre (web novels, traditional literature, foreign works), comparing them with the user's work, creating new outlines, restructuring plot arcs, or refining chapter-level detailed outlines. The agent does NOT rely on fixed rhythm formulas — it extracts actual structural patterns from real novels.\n\nExamples:\n\n- user: "我觉得我这个小说第二卷的节奏太拖了，帮我看看怎么改"\n assistant: "让我用大纲创作者来分析你第二卷的节奏问题并提供改进方案"\n &lt;commentary&gt;Since the user… ([JunePl/unnamed](https://github.com/JunePl/unnamed/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-outline-architect.md) · ★1)
- **novel-outline-planner**: outline 侧亲笔创作专精 agent。MODE=brainstorm 亲笔写灵感卡；MODE=volume_arc_unit 亲笔写卷级大纲单元（骨架/逐卷 ME 池）；MODE=ecas_cluster_brief / cluster_emergence 生成 2-3 张下一故事块走向卡片，所有路径通向大势终点。只给结构骨架与卡片，不写正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-outline-planner.md) · ★13)
- **novel-pacer**: 长篇小说节奏师（策划编辑）。负责爽点密度分析、情感节奏曲线评估、章节拉力诊断。当需要评估稿件节奏是否合适、爽点分布是否合理、情感高潮是否到位时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-pacer.md) · ★6)
- **novel-plotter**: 长篇小说多线叙事师（剧情编辑）。负责伏笔追踪、线索管理、多视角/多时间线叙事梳理。当需要检查伏笔是否有收、多条故事线是否平衡、或剧情线索是否有遗漏时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-plotter.md) · ★6)
- **novel-polish**: 薄路由层 — 详情见模板目录 agents/ ([Kerlewor/narrative_workbench](https://github.com/Kerlewor/narrative_workbench/blob/HEAD/examples/linan_snow_demo/.claude/agents/novel-polish.md) · ★5)
- **novel-polish**: Polish stage / 小说润色。持久会话，同一主会话内持续存活。只做语言二修，不新增事实、hook、剧情事件或角色章末状态。 ([Kerlewor/narrative_workbench](https://github.com/Kerlewor/narrative_workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-polish.md) · ★5)
- **novel-polisher**: 长篇小说润色师（文字编辑）。负责风格统一、语言打磨、消除陈词滥调、提升文字质感，并按八大叙事病灶+通用清单做去 AI 味处理。当需要对正文进行文字层面的润色、统一全书语言风格、或处理文笔问题时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-polisher.md) · ★6)
- **novel-proofreader**: 网络小说校对，负责四重质量检查：设定一致性、逻辑合理性、风格统一性、原创性（抄袭检测）。确保每章质量达标后才能发布。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-proofreader.md))
- **novel-reader**: 长篇小说读者评审（试读读者）。模拟目标读者的真实阅读体验，给出读者视角的主观评分和反馈。当需要预测读者接受度、评估开篇吸引力、或测试情感共鸣强度时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-reader.md) · ★6)
- **novel-reader**: Web小説の想定読者。スポーン時に指定されたペルソナとして ドラフトへのフィードバックと★評価を行う。 ([finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing](https://github.com/finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reader.md) · ★1)
- **novel-reader-proxy**: 读者代理，模拟真实网文读者的反应，评估爽点效果、追更动力、弃文风险。帮助作者从读者角度审视作品。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-reader-proxy.md))
- **novel-reader-simulator**: 读者模拟器——模拟目标读者群的代入感、共情度、可读性。适用于"看读者反应"、"能不能打动读者"、"节奏会不会拖"等场景。 ([wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel](https://github.com/wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/novel-reader-simulator.agent.md))
- **novel-reading-reflector**: 写作后阅读反思 agent。只审整 cluster 草稿，模拟读者通读体验，发现机械检测捞不到的人机感、节奏、POV、互动质感、锁定事实语义冲突（多跳推理）、悬置线推进性等问题。连续 3 轮 0 issue 才允许进入 cluster-save-state。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reading-reflector.md) · ★13)
- **novel-reflector**: 从一个完整 cluster 提炼可复用写作经验，产出 required reflection artifact。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reflector.md) · ★13)
- **novel-repair-supervisor**: Use this agent when you need to coordinate fixing multiple novel generation system issues. This agent acts as the senior QA manager who plans repairs, delegates to specialized agents (dev/editor/qa_auto), validates their outputs, and produces final reports.\n\nExample:\nContext: User reports novel_system has 4 problems: files not generated, poor chapter continuity, fixed character personalities, cliffhanger endings.\nassistant: "我将启动 novel-repair-supervisor agent 来统筹修复工作。该 agent 会制定修复计划、协调 dev/editor/qa_auto 三个子 agent、验收每项成果，最终输出修复报告。"\n\nExample:\nContext: After dev agent fixes code and edit… ([wuhuixian/51xie](https://github.com/wuhuixian/51xie/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-repair-supervisor.md))
- **novel-replica-writer**: 蒸馏复刻草稿 writer。按指定输入亲笔写风格场景或角色声音样本，只产草稿与 Agent 完成回执。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-replica-writer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-research-collector**: 网络小说资料搜集者，负责根据设定或大纲搜索相关资料，为编辑和写手提供事实支撑。使用WebSearch、WebFetch等工具搜集历史背景、专业知识、行业数据、文化习俗等。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-research-collector.md))
- **novel-researcher**: 调研专精 agent。强制联网搜索和抓取，覆盖灵感、走向卡、角色、事实校验等主链路决策前置步骤。必须产出可核验 research artifact，不写正文、不改数据库主体。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-researcher.md) · ★13)
- **novel-researcher**: 长篇小说研究员（资料编辑）。负责资料考证、背景真实性核查、专业领域细节的准确性验证。当需要核查稿件中的真实背景（时代、地域、行业、专业、制度等）时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-researcher.md) · ★6)
- **novel-review**: 小说复核文件处理器，自主扫描小说目录、解析章节范围、分批派发子代理执行各项检查。当用户提供小说目录路径或章节编号时，由 command 路由到此 agent。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了小说目录和检查项 user: "小说复核 ./我的小说/ --角色" assistant: "我来调用 novel-review agent 扫描目录并检查角色一致性。" &lt;commentary&gt; 用户提供了路径和检查项，command 路由到 agent，agent 扫描目录、定位相关文件、派发角色一致性检查子代理。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了章节范围，全面检查 user: "帮我复核 ./我的小说/ 第5-10章" assistant: "我来调用 novel-review agent 扫描目录，分批检查第5-10章的所有维度。" &lt;commentary&gt; 指定章节范围时，agent 扫描目录定位章节文件，分批派发子代理执行全部检查项。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([hestudy/snowflake-fiction](https://github.com/hestudy/snowflake-fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-review.md) · ★37)
- **novel-review**: 薄路由层 — 详情见模板目录 agents/ ([Kerlewor/narrative_workbench](https://github.com/Kerlewor/narrative_workbench/blob/HEAD/examples/linan_snow_demo/.claude/agents/novel-review.md) · ★5)
- **novel-review**: Review stage / 小说审阅。持久会话，同一主会话内持续存活。只出报告，不改正文或状态。可利用会话内跨章视角识别重复性问题和弧光偏差。 ([Kerlewor/narrative_workbench](https://github.com/Kerlewor/narrative_workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-review.md) · ★5)
- **novel-scene-refiner**: 长篇小说场景师（场景编辑）。负责场景描写优化、环境叙事强化、感官细节校准。当需要改善场景的氛围感、增强画面感、或修复过于平淡/过于堆砌的场景描写时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-scene-refiner.md) · ★6)
- **novel-skill-author**: 作者风格 skill 撰写/精修专精 agent。MODE=draft 从作者档与差距报告亲笔撰写 skill_v{N}.md；MODE=patch 读 SkillOpt rollout 轨迹亲笔提出有界 patch 提案。只产 skill 文本与提案，不跑训练、不改数据库、不评分。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-skill-author.md) · ★13)
- **novel-state-tracker**: Cluster 状态梳理员。读取整块正文和当前运行态数据库，产专用 state delta 与独立完成回执；不创作、不评价、不归档实体事实。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-state-tracker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-state-updater**: State extraction for fiction manuscripts. Records situation, characters, knowledge, and inventory after sections pass validation. ([pknull/asha](https://github.com/pknull/asha/blob/HEAD/plugins/write/agents/novel-state-updater.md))
- **novel-summarizer**: 故事块摘要专精 agent。MODE=cluster 读整 cluster 草稿，写 300-400 字 cluster 级摘要 + scene 子摘要 + 关键细节 + 情绪曲线 + 场景级 Appraisal Beat（chain-of-emotion 结构化情绪 STATE）；MODE=volume 卷边界时聚合本卷全部 cluster 摘要产 300-500 字卷级递归摘要（摘要金字塔·source 回溯）。只负责摘要 + 情绪梳理，不改任何数据库文件。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-summarizer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-titler**: 章节标题创作专精 agent。读取标题 brief 与各章正文，按档位与 per-book 风格校准亲笔写全部章标题，落盘 titles JSON 与回执。不改正文、不切章、不回写数据库。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-titler.md) · ★13)
- **novel-translator**: Translates assigned chunks of a Korean web novel (a kt project under novels/&lt;slug&gt;) into English, checks them with the kt toolkit, and reports new terms. Dispatched by the /translate-novel skill with a slug and a list of chunk ids; also used in "repair" mode to fix chunks that failed a check. ([beadvd/ktrans](https://github.com/beadvd/ktrans/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-translator.md))
- **novel-validator-checker**: cluster 违规检查专精 agent。只读 cluster 草稿，跑 audit_hub cluster 审计并输出 repair brief JSON 给 gen_fixer.py；不直接改正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-validator-checker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-voice-checker**: 对话声纹检查专精 agent。只审整 cluster 草稿中的对话是否匹配角色 voice_pack，输出 cluster 级 voice fix brief JSON 给 gen_fixer.py 执行精修。不直接改正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-voice-checker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-world-keeper**: 设定守护者——专长三层世界观（物理/社会/哲学）、闭合性、感官基调、新概念注册。当用户要求"检查设定"、"补充世界观"、"设定有漏洞"时调用。 ([wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel](https://github.com/wangqiqi/cursor-ai-novel/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/novel-world-keeper.agent.md))
- **novel-writer**: 故事块正文创作 agent（v29 · Claude 亲笔）。接 PLAN_ID/STEP/PROJECT/CLUSTER_ID/MODE/RESEARCH_REF 契约，亲笔逐场景写整 cluster 草稿落盘 claude_scenes/，再调 gen_writer.py 做 gemini 分段润色出终稿。本 agent 不切章、不回写 factual 状态。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-writer**: 长篇小说写作者（执笔者）。负责正文写作、场景扩写、章节生成、对白补全。当需要根据大纲生成正文、扩写片段、续写章节、或按指定气质创作时调用。题材中性。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★6)
- **novel-writer**: 薄路由层 — 详情见模板目录 agents/ ([Kerlewor/narrative_workbench](https://github.com/Kerlewor/narrative_workbench/blob/HEAD/examples/linan_snow_demo/.claude/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★5)

- **novel-writer**: Professional fiction-writing agent for story ideation, outlining, characterization, chapter planning, and parallel chapter drafting with subagents. ([hailingu/llm-prompts](https://github.com/hailingu/llm-prompts/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-writer.agent.md) · ★2)

- **NovelOS 上下文管理员**: 整理本轮指定资料与最小上下文，返回有来源的摘要和缺口；市场版不依赖外部服务。仅在明确资料整理任务时使用。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/marketplace/feelfish/.feelfish/agents/novelos-tool-operator.md) · ★3)
- **NovelOS 人物与情感导演**: 设计角色欲望、恐惧、误解、关系博弈和场景情绪节拍，让人物推动事件而不是被情节拖动。重大关系变化、情绪场、角色失真或人物弧设计时调用；不负责硬事实审计和全文润色。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/.feelfish/agents/novelos-character-director.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **NovelOS 市场与读者策略师**: 把最新番茄平台证据、历史作品数据和目标读者需求转成可验证的选题与读者契约。只在新书立项、平台显著变化或连续数据异常时调用；普通章节保持关闭。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/marketplace/feelfish/.feelfish/agents/novelos-market-strategist.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **NovelOS 状态与学习管理员**: 在人工批准或生产态自动验收回执有效后，更新事件、人物、关系、知识来源、时间、物品、伏笔和学习记录；草稿阶段不得提交正式状态。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/.feelfish/agents/novelos-state-curator.md) · ★3)
- **NovelOS 状态与学习管理员**: 用户批准对应章节后提取并保存状态差量，保留历史证据；未批准不更新正式事实。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/marketplace/feelfish/.feelfish/agents/novelos-state-curator.md) · ★3)
- **NovelOS 研究编辑**: 负责网文市场、平台规则、历史与职业考据、时事和网络圈层语境、作品技法研究。查市场、考据、补最新知识、了解圈层表达或拆书时调用；不把未经核验的资料直接写入正文或Canon。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/marketplace/feelfish/.feelfish/agents/novelos-research-editor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **novelty-auditor**: 判断一个研究方案是否已被做过，并单独核验相关论文的发表状态（arXiv 预印本 / 已接收 / 正式发表 / 撤稿 / 未确认）。用于立项前的新颖性审查、审稿时判断贡献边界、或核对"这篇到底发没发出来"；只给带标识符与取数时间的证据，不做推断。 ([RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi](https://github.com/RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/novelty-auditor.md))

- **novelty-checker**: ArXiv novelty sweep. Use at every phase boundary, before submitting any paper, and on request. ([raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis](https://github.com/raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-checker.md))
- **novelty-checker**: Agentic semantic novelty analysis. Determines if code changes represent genuine algorithmic innovation vs. cosmetic rewrites. - **novelty-claim-extractor**: Same-family novelty-claim-extraction agent for the prior-art-search skill. Runs in a fresh, independent context and enumerates the atomic novelty claims of a doc-shaped artifact — the "X is new / first / has no prior art" assertions the search+collision pipeline hunts for uncited prior art against. Distinguishes novelty claims from factual/citation claims (psv's domain) and interpretive claims (escalate to human). Reports a schema'd claim list ONLY — never edits or fixes. Spawn it as the extraction step (NC-I2) of a prior-art-search run. read-only. ([TSQTT-Cyber-Lab/vpu-crm](https://github.com/TSQTT-Cyber-Lab/vpu-crm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-critic.md))
- **novelty-maximizer**: 4단계 Method persona (opus): 선택 gap 에 대해 새로움을 극대화한 후보 3~5개(ARIS idea-creator 브레인스토밍 프롬프트 fork). mode=ideate. mode=scoop 는 ResearchStudio scoop-check 7단계 + idea-quality 채점을 vendor SKILL 경로로 수행. 호출: stage-4-method. - **novelty-picker**: Read experiments/novelty_surface.json and recommend a fresh trio of instruments (deep revivals, family diversity), an unused BPM, a key with half-step modulation, and a scene frame for the next Suno prompt. - **novelty-reviewer**: Reviews an academic manuscript's novelty and positioning by actually searching the literature - extracts the claimed contributions, searches OpenAlex/arXiv for prior and concurrent work, snowballs from the closest hits, and judges whether the claimed delta survives. Use for the novelty lens of a paper review panel or a pre-submission novelty check. ([phamquiluan/dotfiles](https://github.com/phamquiluan/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/novelty-reviewer.md) · ★15)
- **novelty-reviewer**: 新创性审查专家：三性逐项自检、检索指引、驳回风险预判，提交前最后一道自我审查。适用于 patent 命令收尾阶段。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-patent](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-patent/blob/HEAD/agents/novelty-reviewer.md))
- **novelty-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer for novelty claims. Empowered to argue that a proposed feature is already known, uninteresting, or violates the novelty firewall. - **novelty-scout**: Searches for the closest prior and concurrent work to an unpublished ML paper (DBLP, Crossref, OpenAlex via search_references; arXiv, Semantic Scholar, OpenReview via web search), checks the novelty claims and the bibliography against it, and reports the "this was already done in X" risks. - **novelty-screener**: One stretch of the plausibility flow: discuss the stated hypothesis against the scout pool's abstracts, grade each claim's novelty and conceptual plausibility with quoted evidence — every grade provisional until ingestion — rank the top 5 references for the later ingestion stage, and close with one plausibility paragraph on where the proposal stands. Ends at the report; publishes nothing, writes nothing, ingests nothing, and deliberates nothing. ([LIAUNAL/papersmith-ai](https://github.com/LIAUNAL/papersmith-ai/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/novelty-screener.md) · ★8 · 8 copies)
- **novelty-strategist**: Surveys prior work for a research idea or draft AND devises differentiated, novel angles — maps the landscape, finds the gap, flags scoop/concurrent work, then proposes concrete differentiated contributions and the cheapest experiments to establish them. Use when you want not just "what exists" but "what should WE do that's defensibly new." Reusable across papers. ([mehrlander/web-tools](https://github.com/mehrlander/web-tools/blob/HEAD/skills/agents/novice-reader.md) · ★2)
- **novice-reader**: Stage 4 of the /explainer teacher pipeline. - **novice-walker**: Use to find out where a real newcomer would get stuck, by walking a target cold in an isolated subagent that is given only what a newcomer actually has — it attempts one concrete goal under one persona constraint, stops at the first thing it cannot proceed past without information it was not given, and reports the exact stall point plus how many actions each milestone cost against its budget. ([mateonunez/nucleo](https://github.com/mateonunez/nucleo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nucleo-expert.md) · ★2)
- **nucleus-updater**: Detects drift between agent instructions and source of truth (code, files, agent roster). Proposes fixes as multiselect for human approval. Reports only - never auto-edits without consent. ([PEZ/epupp](https://github.com/PEZ/epupp/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/nucleus-updater.agent.md) · ★121)
- **nudge-analyst**: Use this agent when the user wants to evaluate, audit, score, diagnose, or review an existing app, screen, UI flow, retention metrics, or competitor for psychological engagement patterns. Also use when the user provides a screenshot and wants psychological analysis, asks about dark patterns, or wants to understand why users are leaving their app. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/analyst.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-and-coder**: Moved from AGENTS.md `## This app`. Read when working on the Nudge agent, its tools and instructions, or the Cursor coder, change phases, undo, and the Yard deploy wait. ([Bobowski/nudgethis](https://github.com/Bobowski/nudgethis/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/nudge-and-coder.md))
- **nudge-art-director**: Use this agent when the user needs specific, curated color palettes for a product, screen, campaign, or feature. This is the taste-maker — the agent that picks the EXACT shades and says "not THAT teal, THIS teal." Use when the Color Psychologist has defined psychological constraints and someone needs to execute them artistically, or when the user wants a palette that feels like a specific vibe (editorial, gaming, luxury, brutalist, etc.). Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/art-director.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-audit**: Independent CONFIRMATION / AUDIT agent for NUDGE (hardening-loop role 4). Given a UQ fixer's fix CLAIM, it independently re-verifies it WITHOUT trusting the fixer — re-runs the red-team repro (does the confident-wrong now abstain?), the positive control (does it still resolve, i.e. no over-abstention?), and the full gate. Returns a hard PASS/FAIL with evidence. A FAIL kicks the problem back to the UQ fixer. ([NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE](https://github.com/NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nudge-audit.md) · ★1)
- **nudge-brand-identity**: Use this agent when the user needs to create a permanent brand color system — the definitive 5-8 colors their company or product will live in. This is different from picking a palette for one screen or campaign (that's the Art Director). This agent creates the COLOR SYSTEM with primary, secondary, accent, semantic, neutral colors plus usage rules, do's/don'ts, and dark mode guidelines that become the brand bible. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/brand-identity-designer.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-color-psychologist**: Use this agent when the user needs psychologically-informed color decisions for their app or product. This agent is the color psychology specialist — it defines the psychological constraints and guidelines that a designer should work within. Use when the user asks about what colors to use, what emotions colors evoke, what palette fits their brand/category, or wants to analyze their existing colors for psychological impact. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/color-psychologist.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-color-trends**: Use this agent when the user wants to know what colors are trending, what's dated, what's emerging, or wants their palette evaluated for how current it feels. This agent thinks like the Pantone Color Institute and WGSN trend forecasters. Use when the user asks "is this palette still modern?", "what colors are trending in 2026?", "does my app look dated?", or wants to future-proof their color choices. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/color-trend-forecaster.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-designer**: Use this agent when the user is building a new app or feature and wants to design in-app engagement strategy, onboarding flows, gamification, reward mechanics, or viral/referral loops from scratch. For pricing/paywall strategy, defer to the Monetization Architect. For out-of-app retention (push, email, resurrection), defer to the Lifecycle Strategist. For color palettes, defer to the Color Department. Use when the user is creating something new, not evaluating something existing. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/designer.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-jax-physics**: JAX / physics model-builder for NUDGE (hardening-loop role 2 — the seed/builder). Implements a NEW differentiable mathematical model / attribution capability (additive; NEVER touches fit.py or core/), and finds relevant public datasets by spawning a deep-research subagent of ITSELF. Synthetic-first, fail-safe (0 confident-wrong), gated on confirming real data can showcase the effect before investing. ([NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE](https://github.com/NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nudge-jax-physics.md) · ★1)
- **nudge-lifecycle-strategist**: Use this agent when the user needs to design the "out-of-app" experience — push notification strategy, email lifecycle sequences, re-engagement campaigns, resurrection flows for churned users, or "zombie user" win-back strategies. This is the Retention Engine — it owns what happens AFTER the user closes the app. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/lifecycle-strategist.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-monetization-architect**: Use this agent when the user needs to design pricing strategy, paywall architecture, subscription tier structure, trial optimization, or any monetization-related decisions. This is the Revenue Engine — the specialist who knows that the paywall screen generates more revenue per pixel than any other screen in the app. Use when the user asks about pricing, paywalls, free vs. freemium, trial length, subscription tiers, LTV optimization, or conversion psychology. Examples: ([mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin](https://github.com/mermaidsinhell-stack/Nudge-Claude-Code-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/monetization-architect.md) · ★2)
- **nudge-red-team**: Adversarial fail-safe RED-TEAM for NUDGE (hardening-loop role 1). Hunts for CONFIDENT-WRONG holes — a capability emitting a specific positive mechanism/interaction/verdict where the honest answer is abstention, slipping past the abstention gates. It REPORTS and REPRODUCES; it never fixes. Its verdict (holes found, or NONE) drives the loop: if it finds no new holes after a genuine sweep, the loop STOPS. ([NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE](https://github.com/NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nudge-red-team.md) · ★1)
- **nudge-uq-fixer**: Uncertainty-quantification FIXER for NUDGE (hardening-loop role 3). Takes ONE red-team-found confident-wrong problem, independently VALIDATES it (reproduces the hole from scratch), then implements a TARGETED, MEASURED fix — a guard grounded in a measured degeneracy/curvature, not an arbitrary threshold — with tests, a decoy, and the honesty record (a NUDGE-LIM entry + a FINDINGS entry). Fix over confident-wrong is paramount; a well-documented abstention is a valid fix. ([NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE](https://github.com/NicholasEhsanRoy/NUDGE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nudge-uq-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **nudge-ux**: Consultant on user experience, behavioural design and ergonomics — designing flows people actually complete, and nudging them towards the right thing without manipulating them. Use when a flow loses people, when players or admins are confused, when onboarding or joining fails, when deciding what a screen should ask for and in what order, when copy needs to change behaviour, when a control has to work one-handed on a phone in the dark, or when reviewing a proposed feature for friction, silent failure or deceptive patterns. Also use for sludge audits of existing flows and for the ethics check o… ([charlesbaynham/streetfight](https://github.com/charlesbaynham/streetfight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nudge-ux.md) · ★1)

- **nuget-package-updater**: Execution worker for one independent lane of an approved NuGet Hebung plan. Used by the nuget-hebung orchestrator to parallelize disjoint lanes. Does NOT touch the shared Directory.Packages.props unless told it owns that serialized step. - **nuget-project-investigator**: Read-only investigator for a single .NET project during a NuGet Hebung. - **nullness**: `org.jspecify:jspecify` annotations (`@Nullable` / `@NonNull` / `@NullMarked`) are checked by **NullAway**, running as an Error Prone plugin on the Gradle `compileJava` task.
